        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: M7C — Run tests, fix regressions from M7A+M7B

## Overview

**Goal.** M7A ("mechanical cleanup") and M7B ("architectural rewrites — overlay bridge self-sourced `open`, `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed bootstrap effects") both landed in a sandbox where `node_modules/.bin/tsc` and `node_modules/.bin/vitest` were absent, so zero tests were run. M7C runs the full tsc + vitest suite against the current branch (`megaplan/m7-state-cleanup`) and fixes any failures the static analysis and unit tests surface. This is a test-driven fixup phase: **drive the plan by failing output, not speculation.**

**Repo shape that matters.**
- Branch `megaplan/m7-state-cleanup` has a dirty tree (`git status` shows ~21 files modified + `__tests__/` dirs added). The changes are M7A's diagnostic-log strip and M7B's overlay + bootstrap rewrites. Commits `eea6da203` (M7A) and `8c613d041` (M4-era lightbox fix) are the last tracked commits.
- Toolchain is now present: `node_modules/.bin/tsc` and `node_modules/.bin/vitest` both exist at this checkout (verified). The M7B evidence files explicitly record that tsc/vitest were skipped.
- New test files staged but never executed:
  - `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/__tests__/select.closed.test.tsx` — pointer-events-stranding regression for unopened `<Select>`.
  - `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — 6 cases for `dataUpdatedAt`-keyed bootstrap.
  - `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` — 5 cases for lightbox bootstrap.
- Modified existing tests (`overlayBridge.test.tsx`, `lightbox.test.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx`) added `waitFor` wrappers because the bridge now registers asynchronously (MutationObserver must observe `data-state`).
- `useToolSettings.ts` added a required `dataUpdatedAt: number` to both overload return types. This is the only public-API change in M7B and ripples through 11+ Group A callers.

**Constraints.**
- Keep scope to fixing whatever the test run surfaces from M7A+M7B work. Do NOT revert unrelated changes (memory: `feedback_never_revert_unrelated_changes.md`).
- Do NOT touch the known deferred M7C mirror site at `src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts:128-195` unless a failing test drives a minimal change there.
- Prefer fixing tests + production code (per CLAUDE.md: "Fix the problem fully. Don't limit scope just to avoid breaking existing tests — update the tests too if needed.").
- Preserve the M7A/M7B invariants: `isOverlayActuallyOpen` stays deleted, `lastBootstrapRef` stays deleted, `open` stays required on `UseOverlayBridgeOptions`, `dataUpdatedAt` stays in bootstrap-effect deps with RQ-result-object identity excluded.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Verify toolchain + capture baseline failures
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** `ls node_modules/.bin/tsc node_modules/.bin/vitest` both exist (already confirmed).
2. **Run** `npx tsc --noEmit` and save output to `/tmp/m7c-tsc.log`. Expect failures rippling from `useToolSettings` return-type change, destructure mismatches in the 11 Group A callers, or unrelated pre-existing errors.
3. **Run** the targeted M7B surface: `npx vitest run src/shared/components/ui/overlay src/shared/settings/hooks src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks src/shared/hooks/settings --reporter=verbose`, save to `/tmp/m7c-vitest-targeted.log`.
4. **Run** full `npx vitest run --reporter=verbose` once the targeted run settles, save to `/tmp/m7c-vitest-full.log`.
5. **Summarize** failures into three buckets: (a) TS compile errors, (b) M7B-introduced test failures, (c) unrelated pre-existing failures (leave these alone — out of scope for M7C).

### Step 2: Fix TypeScript errors from `useToolSettings` ripple (`src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts`, caller files surfaced in Step 1)
**Scope:** Medium (depends on how many callers break)
1. **Inspect** each caller surfaced in the tsc log. The M7B meta called out 11 RQ-backed Group A callers (`useCharacterAnimateSettings`, `useImageGenGallery`, `useShotSettings`, `useJoinSegmentsSettings`, `usePersistentToolState`, `useOutputSelection`, `useStructureVideo`, `useEditVideoSettings`, `useAudio`, `usePersistentPromptSettings`, `useShotManagement`) plus `useLastUsedEditSettings` — they inherit `dataUpdatedAt` automatically via destructure, so the common fix is either (a) no change needed (ignored field), or (b) update a downstream type if a wrapper re-declares the return shape.
2. **Fix** each error by the simplest means: either add `dataUpdatedAt` to downstream wrapper types, or destructure+discard with a rest pattern. Do NOT re-add `isOverlayActuallyOpen`, `lastBootstrapRef`, or other deleted surface.
3. **Re-run** `npx tsc --noEmit` until clean. If an error is clearly pre-existing (not related to M7A/M7B changes), note it in the summary and skip — it's out of M7C scope.

### Step 3: Fix overlay test failures (`src/shared/components/ui/overlay/**`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **For each failing overlay test**, diagnose by priority:
   - **Timing / MutationObserver**: Base UI may render `SelectContent`/`PopoverContent` inside a conditional Portal. Bridge now depends on popup DOM with `data-state="open"` to set `open=true`. If the test asserts stack contents synchronously, wrap the assertion in `waitFor`. (The M7B diff already added `waitFor` to the known sites; if more are needed, add them.)
   - **`open: boolean` required**: Any direct `useOverlayBridge({type, ...})` call missing `open` will error at compile time — fix at call site with `open: true` (or a controlled boolean for open/closed fixtures). The M7B diff updated `overlayBridge.test.tsx:77` and `:200`; any missed caller will show up.
   - **`select.closed.test.tsx` regression**: If this fails, the bridge is still registering an unopened Select. Root cause candidates: (a) `useOpenFromDataState` returning `true` for a closed popup because Base UI sets `data-state="closed"` vs missing the attribute (check `readOpenState` at `useOpenFromDataState.ts:3`); (b) `registerElement` being called before `setPopupEl` triggers rerender (check `composeRefs` order in `select.tsx:203-211` — `setPopupEl` must run before/alongside `registerElement`). Fix by tightening `readOpenState` to strict-equal or reordering the composeRefs chain.
   - **`overlaySequences.test.tsx`** (pointer-events through open/close): If close transitions don't pop the overlay, the MutationObserver isn't firing on `data-state` changes before React unmounts the popup. Remedy: rely on the bridge's unmount cleanup at `overlayBridge.tsx:193-207` (already preserved) — no code change needed; may need a `waitFor` adjustment.
2. **Capture** one short inline comment per fix explaining *why* the assertion is async (MutationObserver subscription timing) — but only if the reason isn't obvious.
3. **Do not** reintroduce `isOverlayActuallyOpen`, `offsetParent` checks, or DOM-attribute reads in `overlayStack.ts` — the reverted `getTopModalOverlay` is correct per M7B decision.

### Step 4: Fix bootstrap test failures (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`, `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **For each failing bootstrap assertion**, diagnose against the ideal contract the M7B plan codified (`dataUpdatedAt`-keyed, RQ-identity out of deps):
   - **"Fires exactly once on `isLoading` true→false" fails with 0 or 2 calls**: Check the effect body in `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:279-319`. The early-return branches (`!isEntityValid`, `authoritativeIsLoading`, `authoritativeError`) must not incorrectly block the first post-load firing. The deps include `authoritativeUpdatedAt`; the test advances it from `0→1` alongside `isLoading true→false`, so the effect re-runs and exits the early-return.
   - **"Identity-stable rerenders fire zero additional" fails with extra calls**: A non-scalar ended up in the dep array. Grep the effect deps for `authoritativeSettings`, `bootstrapData`, or `store` identity. If present, remove and read via ref (`authoritativeSettingsRef`, `bootstrapDataRef`) — the pattern already exists at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:235-238`.
   - **"`dataUpdatedAt` advance re-bootstraps" fails with 1 call**: The deps array is missing `authoritativeUpdatedAt` — verify line 316 still lists it.
   - **"custom-mode with stable entityId does not re-bootstrap" fails with 2 calls**: Custom-mode branch is reading `bootstrapData` from closure instead of `bootstrapDataRef.current`. Verify line 285 reads from the ref.
   - **Mock setup issue: `Object.assign(store.getState(), { bootstrapEntity: bootstrapSpy })` doesn't survive**: Zustand's `set()` spreads state into a new object, so the mutated spy should carry forward. If the spy count is always 0, the hook may be reading `bootstrapEntity` from a stale closure. Pull `bootstrapEntity = storeState.bootstrapEntity` fresh in each render (already done at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:225`). If still broken, swap the mock to override `bootstrapEntity` via `store.setState({ bootstrapEntity: spy })` instead of in-place `Object.assign`.
2. **For `useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` failures**: same diagnosis but against `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:159-173`. The effect body reads `dbSettingsRef.current` and keys on `[bootstrapEntity, dbUpdatedAt, entityId, hasPersistedData]`.
3. **When a test expectation is wrong** (e.g., an assertion that the spy was called with a specific clone that the implementation can't produce exactly), update the test rather than distorting the production code — the M7B bootstrap contract is the invariant, not the specific call-site shape. Justify any test mutation in a one-line inline comment.

### Step 5: Re-run the full verification ladder
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `npx tsc --noEmit` → must exit 0.
2. **Run** `npx vitest run src/shared/components/ui/overlay src/shared/settings/hooks src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks src/shared/hooks/settings --reporter=verbose` → must report 0 failures in the targeted scope.
3. **Run** full `npx vitest run` → M7A+M7B-related failures must be 0; unrelated pre-existing failures documented but not blocking.
4. **Re-verify** the M7B static evidence gates still pass (spot check the `rg` commands from `evidence_static.txt`): `isOverlayActuallyOpen` still absent, `lastBootstrapRef` still absent, `dataUpdatedAt` still in `useToolSettings.ts`, `open: boolean` still required in `overlayBridge.tsx`.

### Step 6: Record outcomes
**Scope:** Small
1. **Summarize** in the commit message: count of tsc errors fixed, count of tests fixed, and any pre-existing failures explicitly excluded (with one-line reason each).
2. **Do not** create new `.md` docs (per CLAUDE.md: "Don't create new `*.md` unless explicitly requested"). Append to `state_ownership.md` only if an invariant changed — which it shouldn't for a fixup phase.

## Execution Order
1. Capture baseline failures first (Step 1) — plan shape depends on what actually fails.
2. Fix tsc errors before test errors (Step 2 before 3+4) — tests can't even load if compile fails.
3. Fix overlay and bootstrap tests in parallel (Steps 3 and 4 are independent).
4. Full re-run at the end (Step 5).
5. Record outcomes (Step 6).

## Validation Order
1. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors.
2. Targeted `npx vitest run` on the four M7B directories → 0 failures.
3. Full `npx vitest run` → 0 new failures introduced by M7A+M7B (pre-existing unrelated failures may remain).
4. `__resetOverlayStackForTests()` cleanup between tests keeps body.pointerEvents pristine (sanity-check via `rg '__resetOverlayStackForTests' src/shared/components/ui/overlay`).

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-132",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 11.2 specifies the reverse switch is 'single commit, no `start`/`children`/`offset` reads anywhere' but doesn't state the mutation type explicitly. since the step describes metadeletes + cliporderoverride + pinnedshotgroupsoverride in a single commit, the mutation must be `type: 'rows'` (the only variant carrying all three). self-consistent with step 11.1's restriction on `type: 'pinnedshotgroups'`, but making the mutation type explicit would reduce executor ambiguity.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-135",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 3 against the actual `useselectedmediaclips()` inputs in [useselectedmediaclips.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). that hook currently reads only `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig` from editor context, so the plan's literal instruction to join `resolvedconfig.pinnedshotgroups` with `useshots()` data will not compile unless it first switches to `data?.config.pinnedshotgroups` or another real source of pinned groups.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-149",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual timeline selection ownership across `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelineselection.ts`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usemultiselect.ts`. `usetimelinecommit` owns only `selectedclipid` and `selectedtrackid`, while `usemultiselect` still owns `selectedclipids`, `primaryclipid`, and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan's step 3 is technically incomplete: following it literally would leave timeline selection split between the new store and old hook-local state instead of collapsing it into one authoritative store.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-150",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the current imperative selection adapters in `useclipdrag.ts`, `usetimelinepersistence.ts`, `usepollsync.ts`, and `videoeditorprovider.tsx`. the mutable refs (`selectedclipidsref`, `additiveselectionref`, `selectedclipidref`, `selectedtrackidref`) are runtime adapters for drag/save/poll callers rather than durable domain state, so the plan's wording about putting 'refs/helpers' into the `timeline` slice is a design error unless it explicitly keeps store state separate from ref adapters rebuilt around that state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-166",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` and `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/tracklistrenderer.tsx`. `timelinecanvas` still computes shared `rowresizepreview`, `selectedtrackid`, and other drag-sensitive props centrally and passes them through every `sortablerow`, so moving context reads to narrow zustand selectors alone will not meet the gate that non-dragged rows stay at 0 rerenders during drag unless the plan also changes this row-prop fan-out.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-167",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the staged-vs-mounted add flow in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that hook decides between immediate in-process drop and staged url/localstorage fallback by testing whether the safe timeline hooks return `null`, so a global `timelinestore.ts` with default state would change runtime behavior unless the migration preserves an explicit 'editor mounted' availability boundary.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-168",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts` and `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/videoeditorprovider.tsx` together. the current composition is synchronous and consumers like the pending-add drain and agentchat bridge read fresh `editor` values in the same render pass, so the plan's 'build values and write them into the zustand store' step is under-specified about whether hydration happens during render or in an effect, and either choice has real correctness risk if not designed carefully.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      ]
    },

      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      ]

      "id": "DEBT-175",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the real callers of the safe timeline hooks in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that code passes through both mounted-editor values and the `null` outside-provider case, then uses `ops.registergenerationasset`, `ops.handleassetdrop`, and `data.resolvedconfig?.clips` when available, so any replacement api has to preserve both nullable access and the current immediate-drop contract rather than only exposing non-null store selectors.",

      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",

- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

Those preservation checks are aligned with the repository, even though they are not sufficient to resolve the current failures on their own.","flagged":false}]},{"id":"callers","question":"Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?","findings":[{"detail":"I grepped `useOverlayBridge(` and every live component caller already passes an explicit `open` boolean: `lightbox.tsx`, `select.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx`, `dialog.tsx`, `tooltip.tsx`, `hover-card.tsx`, `popover.tsx`, and `alert-dialog.tsx`. The specific failing submenu path is `DropdownMenuContent` and `DropdownMenuSubContent` in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.tsx:84-95` and `:148-159`, and both already derive `open` from DOM state. That means the plan's caller-oriented bullet about missed `open` arguments does not address the actual failing call sites.","flagged":true},{"detail":"I grepped `getTopmostKnownModalOverlayType(` and the only runtime caller is `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxNavigation.ts:94`. The input it effectively receives is not just an overlay type string; it is whatever `overlayStack` considers the top modal entry, including entries with `modal: true` and no elements, as shown by the failing stale-select test. The fix therefore belongs in the callee side of the overlay helper or stack semantics, not in `useLightboxNavigation` event wiring alone, and the current plan does not spell that out.","flagged":true},{"detail":"I also checked the `useLastUsedEditSettings` caller chain with `rg 'useLastUsedEditSettings\\(' src`. The live product caller is `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:87-97`, which feeds `lastUsedSettings.lastUsed` straight into `useGenerationEditSettings` as `bootstrapSettings`. That explains why the current merge/bootstrap bug surfaces in `useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts` even though the root hook under test is `useLastUsedEditSettings`; the plan needs to treat that caller chain as part of the same fix surface.","flagged":true}]}],"flags":[{"id":"FLAG-001","concern":"M7C completeness: the plan does not cover three of the six currently reproduced failing files (`useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts`, `useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts`, and `useLightboxNavigation.test.ts`), so executing it as written would leave known red tests outside the worklist.","category":"completeness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"The idea file names six failing files, and `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage` reproduced 15 failures across exactly those six. In code, `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:121-139` returns an empty load result for React Query mode, `createEntityStore.ts:398-430` bootstraps that seed via `ensureEntity()`, and `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:279-318` bootstraps again from query data."},{"id":"FLAG-003","concern":"Overlay gating correctness: the plan misses the actual stale-modal suppression path because `overlayDom.ts` currently returns the top registered modal overlay without verifying that it is open, which is exactly what the failing `useLightboxNavigation` test exercises.","category":"correctness","severity_hint":"likely-significant","evidence":"`overlayDom.ts:81-88` claims to return the topmost actually-open modal overlay but just proxies `getTopModalOverlay()?.type`, and `getTopModalOverlay()` in `overlayStack.ts:88-95` does not filter empty or unopened overlays."}],"verified_flag_ids":[],"disputed_flag_ids":[]}

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: M7C — Fix the 15 actual test failures

## Overview

**The gate reproduced the failure set exactly**, so the plan is now anchored to concrete root causes rather than speculation. `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage` currently reports **15 failures across 6 files** — the exact set the idea doc names:

| # | File | Failures | Root cause bucket |
|---|------|---------|-------------------|
| 1 | `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` | 6 | Double-bootstrap |
| 2 | `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` | 5 | Double-bootstrap |
| 3 | `src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts` | 1 | Double-bootstrap + legacy mock missing `dataUpdatedAt` |
| 4 | `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts` | 1 | Double-bootstrap + legacy mock uses `null` where `undefined` is required by the `hasPersistedData = dbSettings !== undefined` contract |
| 5 | `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.test.tsx` | 1 | Overlay stack registration order inverted (`['menu-submenu','menu']`) because child effects fire before parent effects |
| 6 | `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxNavigation.test.ts` | 1 | `getTopModalOverlay()` does not filter stale modal entries (`elements.length === 0`) |

**tsc is already clean** (verified). The original Step 2 (TS-ripple speculation) is deleted.

**Two real root causes**:

**(A) Double-bootstrap through `createEntityStore.ensureEntity`.** Confirmed trace: `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:223` calls `store.useEntity(activeEntityId)`, which at `createEntityStore.ts:593-595` runs `ensureEntity(entityId)`. ensureEntity at `:398-430` always calls `config.load(entityId)` and then `bootstrapEntity(...)` with the result. For the RQ-mode hook, `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:121-139` returns `{ db: null, lastUsed: null }` from load — so ensureEntity fires a bootstrap with a null seed. The explicit effect at `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:279-318` then fires a second bootstrap with the real RQ data. Spy counts double; the shot-change regression in `useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts` also fails because the async ensureEntity bootstrap resolves **after** the explicit bootstrap and overwrites the real settings with defaults. Same shape in `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` (load returns `{db:null, lastUsed: localSettings}`; explicit effect merges db+local).

**(B) Overlay stack order + stale-modal filtering.** Confirmed at `overlayDom.ts:87-88` (`getTopmostKnownModalOverlayType()` proxies `getTopModalOverlay()?.type`) and `overlayStack.ts:88-97` (`getTopModalOverlay` reverse-iterates for `overlay.modal === true` with zero element-presence check). The dropdown submenu ordering bug is separate: when nested overlays both register via React effects, child effects fire before parent effects, so submenu pushes first → stack = `[submenu, menu]` instead of `[menu, submenu]`. Fix at push time: insertion-sort by `compareDocumentPosition` of popup elements.

**Constraints.**
- Fix root causes in the **shared** layers (`createEntityStore.ts`, `overlayStack.ts`) — not per-hook workarounds.
- Preserve M7B invariants: `isOverlayActuallyOpen` stays deleted, `lastBootstrapRef` stays deleted, `open: boolean` stays required, `dataUpdatedAt` stays in the bootstrap-effect deps for `useAutoSaveSettings` and `useLastUsedEditSettings`.
- Deferred `useJoinClipsSettings.ts:128-195` is **not** in the failing set — leave it alone.
- Use the idea-file required evidence command: `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage 2>&1 | tail -5` and show `0 failed`.

## Phase 1: Reproduce + confirm

### Step 1: Reproduce the failure set (`./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage 2>&1 | tail -50` to confirm 15 failures across the six listed files before touching code.
2. **Verify** `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` is still clean (gate already confirmed). No changes from M7C should regress TS.
3. **Capture** per-file pre-fix failure counts into `/tmp/m7c-baseline.log` for the commit-message summary.

## Phase 2: Fix double-bootstrap through `ensureEntity` (covers failures in files 1–4)

### Step 2: Make `config.load` opt out of the auto-bootstrap path (`src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Widen** `EntityStoreConfig<T>.load` return type to allow `null`: `(entityId: string) => Promise<{ db: T | null; lastUsed: T | null } | null>`.
2. **Update** `ensureEntity` at `createEntityStore.ts:398-430`: after `config.load(entityId)` resolves, if `result === null`, do not call `bootstrapEntity`. Instead, set the entity's status back to `'idle'` (clearing the `'loading'` state set at `:409-421`) and return — the caller's explicit effect is authoritative.
   ```ts
   const loadPromise = config.load(entityId).then((result) => {
     if (result === null) {
       set((state) => {
         const existing = state.entities[entityId];
         if (!existing) return state;
         return {
           entities: {
             ...state.entities,
             [entityId]: { ...existing, status: 'idle', error: null },
           },
         };
       });
       return;
     }
     get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db: result.db, lastUsed: result.lastUsed });
   });
   ```
3. **Update** `reloadEntity` at `createEntityStore.ts:563` (and `:572`) symmetrically — when `load()` returns null, do not bootstrap; preserve whatever the caller most recently seeded.
4. **Rationale comment** (one line): `// null signals the caller will bootstrap explicitly; avoids double-fire with RQ-mode effects`.

### Step 3: Switch RQ-mode hooks to null-load (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** at `:117-163`: in the `load` function, after the `DISABLED_ENTITY_ID` branch, when `runtime.mode === 'react-query'` return `null` (instead of `{ db: null, lastUsed: null }`). Keep the `custom` branch returning real data.
2. **`useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`** at `:95-98`: replace the current `load: async (entityId) => ({ db: null, lastUsed: ... })` with `load: async () => null`. The explicit effect at `:159-173` is the authoritative bootstrap (it already reads `readLocalStorageSettings(entityId)` + merges DB data).
3. **Verify** `useJoinClipsSettings.ts:55` (third `createEntityStore` caller) is unaffected: its `load` returns real data, no change needed.

### Step 4: Fix legacy test mocks that predate `dataUpdatedAt` (`src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts`**: each `(useToolSettings as ...).mockImplementation(...)` or `.mockReturnValue(...)` must now include `dataUpdatedAt: <number>` (e.g., `Date.now()` or a monotonic counter) alongside the existing fields. Without it, the hook destructures `undefined` and the `dataUpdatedAt` effect-dep coarsens. Update every mock site (grep `useToolSettings as unknown as` in that file; ~10 call sites per the earlier `grep -n`). Use `1` for stable tests and bump per rerender where the test advances state.
2. **`useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts`**: the mock at `useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts:89-96` returns `settings: null`. The M7B contract is `settings: T | undefined` (never `null`). Change the initial `settings: null` → `settings: undefined`, and add `dataUpdatedAt: 0` (or a counter). Any subsequent `toolSettingsState = { ...toolSettingsState, ...}` updates should bump `dataUpdatedAt` alongside new `settings`.
3. **Do not** tighten `hasPersistedData` from `!== undefined` to `!= null` in production code — the M7B decision chose strict `!== undefined` intentionally (per `useToolSettings` contract and `state_ownership.md`). Fix the mock, not the contract.

## Phase 3: Fix overlay regressions (covers failures in files 5–6)

### Step 5: Filter stale modals in `getTopModalOverlay` (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `getTopModalOverlay` at `overlayStack.ts:88-97` so that a "modal" only counts if it has a real popup element that is actually open. Rule: reverse-iterate overlays and return the last one where `overlay.modal === true` **and** `overlay.elements.some(el => el.getAttribute?.('data-state') === 'open' || el.dataset?.state === 'open')`. Empty-element modals and `data-state="closed"`/missing modals are skipped.
   ```ts
   function getTopModalOverlay(overlays) {
     for (let i = overlays.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
       const o = overlays[i];
       if (!o.modal) continue;
       if (o.elements.length === 0) continue;
       const anyOpen = o.elements.some((el) => el.getAttribute('data-state') === 'open');
       if (anyOpen) return o;
     }
     return null;
   }
   ```
2. **Verify** `overlayDom.ts:87-88` (`getTopmostKnownModalOverlayType`) picks up the new behavior automatically — it just proxies `getTopModalOverlay()?.type`.
3. **Check** `syncBodyPointerEvents` at `overlayStack.ts:121-147`: it also calls `getTopModalOverlay` for pointer-events gating. The tightened filter is correct for that use case too (stale modals should not strand body pointer-events), but **verify the two `overlaySequences.test.tsx` cases still pass** — they mount real popups with `data-state="open"`, so they should remain green.

### Step 6: Fix nested-overlay stack order via document-position sort (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Root cause** is React effect order: children run effects before parents, so a submenu's `useOverlayBridge` registers before its parent's. Push order ≠ DOM order. Fix at insert time by sorting `overlays` so the stack is ordered by popup document position (earlier-in-DOM = earlier-in-stack; topmost = last = later-in-DOM).
2. **Update** `pushOverlay` at `overlayStack.ts:173-183`: after building `nextOverlays`, sort in place using `compareDocumentPosition` on each overlay's first popup-kind element (fallback to first element if none is popup-kind). Entries without any connected element keep their push-order position at the end. Keep the `syncBodyPointerEvents` call sequence.
   ```ts
   nextOverlays.sort((a, b) => compareOverlayDomOrder(a, b));
   ```
   where `compareOverlayDomOrder(a, b)` returns `-1/0/1` based on `aEl.compareDocumentPosition(bEl)` (`DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING` → a before b; `DOCUMENT_POSITION_PRECEDING` → b before a).
3. **Also update** `updateOverlay` at `:185-209` to re-apply the sort after patching `elements` (so a late-arriving element repositions the overlay).
4. **Verify** the single-overlay tests (`lightbox.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlayBridge.test.tsx`) and the sibling two-overlay sequences (`overlaySequences.test.tsx`) still pass — they don't share DOM ancestry, so `compareDocumentPosition` reflects portal-mount order which is the same as push order.

## Phase 4: Verify end-to-end

### Step 7: Targeted + full test run with idea-file evidence format
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLastUsedEditSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.test.tsx src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxNavigation.test.ts --no-coverage 2>&1 | tail -30` — must show **0 failed** across all six files.

4. **Spot-check** the M7B invariants via grep: `rg 'isOverlayActuallyOpen' src` (empty), `rg 'lastBootstrapRef' src` (empty), `rg 'dataUpdatedAt' src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts` (≥1), `rg 'open:\s*boolean' src/shared/components/ui/overlay/overlayBridge.tsx` (≥1).
5. **Capture** the `tail -5` output and the per-file targeted-run outputs into the commit message.

## Execution Order
1. **Step 1** (reproduce) before any code changes — baseline.
2. **Step 2** (createEntityStore) before Step 3 (hook load functions) — widening the return type first prevents TS breakage mid-edit.
3. **Step 3** before **Step 4** — switching load() to null may change some test expectations, so fix production path first, then legacy mocks.
4. **Steps 5 and 6** (overlay) are independent of Steps 2–4 and can run in parallel.
5. **Step 7** (verify) last.

## Validation Order
1. After Step 2+3: `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__ src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/__tests__ --no-coverage` → new bootstrap tests green (11 failures → 0).
2. After Step 4: `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts --no-coverage` → legacy settings tests green (2 failures → 0).

4. After Step 7: `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --no-coverage 2>&1 | tail -5` → **0 failed** (idea-file evidence).

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 3 against the actual `useselectedmediaclips()` inputs in [useselectedmediaclips.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). that hook currently reads only `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig` from editor context, so the plan's literal instruction to join `resolvedconfig.pinnedshotgroups` with `useshots()` data will not compile unless it first switches to `data?.config.pinnedshotgroups` or another real source of pinned groups.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-149",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual timeline selection ownership across `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelineselection.ts`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usemultiselect.ts`. `usetimelinecommit` owns only `selectedclipid` and `selectedtrackid`, while `usemultiselect` still owns `selectedclipids`, `primaryclipid`, and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan's step 3 is technically incomplete: following it literally would leave timeline selection split between the new store and old hook-local state instead of collapsing it into one authoritative store.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-150",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the current imperative selection adapters in `useclipdrag.ts`, `usetimelinepersistence.ts`, `usepollsync.ts`, and `videoeditorprovider.tsx`. the mutable refs (`selectedclipidsref`, `additiveselectionref`, `selectedclipidref`, `selectedtrackidref`) are runtime adapters for drag/save/poll callers rather than durable domain state, so the plan's wording about putting 'refs/helpers' into the `timeline` slice is a design error unless it explicitly keeps store state separate from ref adapters rebuilt around that state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-166",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` and `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/tracklistrenderer.tsx`. `timelinecanvas` still computes shared `rowresizepreview`, `selectedtrackid`, and other drag-sensitive props centrally and passes them through every `sortablerow`, so moving context reads to narrow zustand selectors alone will not meet the gate that non-dragged rows stay at 0 rerenders during drag unless the plan also changes this row-prop fan-out.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-167",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the staged-vs-mounted add flow in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that hook decides between immediate in-process drop and staged url/localstorage fallback by testing whether the safe timeline hooks return `null`, so a global `timelinestore.ts` with default state would change runtime behavior unless the migration preserves an explicit 'editor mounted' availability boundary.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-168",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts` and `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/videoeditorprovider.tsx` together. the current composition is synchronous and consumers like the pending-add drain and agentchat bridge read fresh `editor` values in the same render pass, so the plan's 'build values and write them into the zustand store' step is under-specified about whether hydration happens during render or in an effect, and either choice has real correctness risk if not designed carefully.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "occurrence_count": 1,

Updated [critique_output.json](/private/tmp/reigh-chain-workspace/reigh-app/.megaplan/plans/m7c-test-fixup/critique_output.json) for iteration 2.

The revised plan does resolve the earlier completeness problems, so I marked `FLAG-001` and `FLAG-002` as verified. I re-opened overlay concerns in two forms:

- `FLAG-003`: the stale-modal filter is still too narrow because the repo’s actual open-state contract accepts both `data-state="open"` and Base UI’s `data-open` markers.
- `FLAG-004`: sorting the whole overlay stack by DOM position would change `overlayStack`’s documented ordering semantics for every overlay, not just the failing nested submenu case.

I also added per-check findings explaining those risks and noted that the `createEntityStore.load -> null` idea is plausible, but the plan should account for the shared store’s direct test surface.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: M7C — Fix the 15 actual test failures

## Overview

**The plan is targeting the right code and root causes** — the gate confirmed the settings, overlay-modal, and overlay-ordering diagnoses. Iteration 3 narrows two overlay changes to avoid scope creep and one shared-store change to include its own test suite in the verification surface:

- **Open-state detection** now mirrors the repo's existing `readOpenState()` in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/useOpenFromDataState.ts:3-6`, which accepts **both** `data-state === 'open'` AND `data-open === '' | 'true'`. A filter that checks only `data-state` would misclassify real Base UI popups (dropdown/popover/select popups advertise themselves via `data-open=""`).
- **Overlay stack ordering** no longer touches `updateOverlay`. In `pushOverlay`, the insertion index is computed via `compareDocumentPosition` of the new popup vs. existing popups. For sibling overlays whose popups mount in push-order (every case today — dialog+popover, menu+dialog, lightbox pairs), the new popup is DOM-later → appended at the top → **behavior unchanged**. Only nested overlays whose React child-effects fire before parent-effects (the failing `[submenu, menu]` case) get reordered into DOM order. This preserves the store's documented "top = most recently pushed and open" contract for every caller that currently depends on it.
- **Shared-store test surface** (`src/shared/state/createEntityStore.test.ts`) is now explicitly verified after widening `EntityStoreConfig<T>.load` to return `{db, lastUsed} | null`. The existing test load functions all return objects (unchanged behavior); a new case confirms the new null-skip branch.

**Failure inventory (unchanged from v2) — 15 failures across the 6 idea-file-named files:**

| # | File | Fail | Root cause |
|---|------|------|------------|
| 1 | `src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.bootstrap.test.tsx` | 6 | Double-bootstrap |

| 3 | `src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts` | 1 | Double-bootstrap + legacy mock missing `dataUpdatedAt` |
| 4 | `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts` | 1 | Double-bootstrap + legacy mock `settings: null` vs `undefined` |
| 5 | `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/dropdown-menu.test.tsx` | 1 | Nested registration order (child-effect-before-parent-effect) |
| 6 | `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLightboxNavigation.test.ts` | 1 | `getTopModalOverlay()` doesn't filter stale/closed modals |

**Constraints (unchanged).**
- Fix root causes in shared layers (`createEntityStore.ts`, `overlayStack.ts`).
- Preserve M7B invariants.
- Do not touch `useJoinClipsSettings.ts:128-195` (settled deferral).

## Phase 2: Fix double-bootstrap through `ensureEntity` (covers failures in files 1–4)

### Step 2: Widen `config.load` to opt out of auto-bootstrap (`src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Widen** `EntityStoreConfig<T>.load` return type: `(entityId: string) => Promise<{ db: T | null; lastUsed: T | null } | null>`.
2. **Update** `ensureEntity` at `createEntityStore.ts:398-430`: when `await config.load(entityId)` resolves to `null`, do not call `bootstrapEntity`. Instead, clear the `'loading'` status (set at `:409-421`) back to `'idle'` with `error: null` and return — the caller's explicit effect is authoritative. Keep the loaded-already short-circuit (`current?.loaded`) and the inFlight-dedupe logic unchanged.
   ```ts
   const loadPromise = config.load(entityId).then((result) => {
     if (result === null) {
       set((state) => {
         const existing = state.entities[entityId];
         if (!existing) return state;
         return {
           entities: { ...state.entities, [entityId]: { ...existing, status: 'idle', error: null } },
         };
       });
       return;
     }
     get().bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db: result.db, lastUsed: result.lastUsed });
   });
   ```
3. **Update** `reloadEntity` at `:563`/`:572` symmetrically: when `load()` returns `null`, skip the bootstrap call; preserve whatever the caller most recently seeded.
4. **Rationale comment** (one line): `// null signals the caller will bootstrap explicitly; avoids double-fire with RQ-mode effects`.

### Step 3: Add a shared-store test case for the null-load path (`src/shared/state/createEntityStore.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** the existing cases at `:12-31` (factory helper returning `{db:null, lastUsed:null}`) and `:78-103` (real-data load) still pass — the widened return type is a superset, their `load` fns still return objects.
2. **Add** a new `it(...)` case: `'skips auto-bootstrap when load returns null'`. Assert that a store configured with `load: vi.fn(async () => null)` does NOT populate an entity's `settings` via `bootstrapEntity` when `useEntity(id)` mounts, but does clear the `'loading'` status to `'idle'`. Use `renderHook(() => store.useEntity('null-load'))` and `waitFor(() => expect(store.getState().entities['null-load']?.status).toBe('idle'))`.
3. **Re-run** `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/shared/state/createEntityStore.test.ts --no-coverage` → must be fully green (existing 8 cases + 1 new = 9 cases, 0 failed).

### Step 4: Switch RQ-mode hooks to null-load (`src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`useAutoSaveSettings.ts`** at `:117-163`: in the `load` function, after the `DISABLED_ENTITY_ID` branch and **before** the `runtime.mode === 'custom'` branch, add: `if (runtime.mode === 'react-query') return null;`. Keep the custom branch's existing logic returning real data.
2. **`useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`** at `:95-98`: replace `load: async (entityId) => ({ db: null, lastUsed: ... })` with `load: async () => null`. The explicit effect at `:159-173` (reads `readLocalStorageSettings(entityId)` + merges DB) is the authoritative bootstrap.
3. **Verify** `useJoinClipsSettings.ts:55` (third `createEntityStore` caller) is unaffected: its `load` returns real data, no change.
4. **Note on `useEditSettingsPersistence` chain:** `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:87-97` feeds `useLastUsedEditSettings` output straight into `useGenerationEditSettings`. Once the double-bootstrap is fixed, the merge assertion in `useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts` will reflect clean single-bootstrap output — no additional change to the persistence hook needed (verify via the targeted run).

### Step 5: Fix legacy test mocks that predate `dataUpdatedAt` (`src/shared/hooks/settings/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts`, `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts`**: every `(useToolSettings as unknown as ...)`-shaped mock (about 10 call sites) must include `dataUpdatedAt: <number>` alongside the existing fields. Use `1` for stable cases; bump per rerender where the test advances state (e.g., `shot-1` → `shot-2` should bump from `1` → `2`).
2. **`useGenerationEditSettings.test.ts`**: at `:89-96` change initial `settings: null` → `settings: undefined` and add `dataUpdatedAt: 0`. Where `toolSettingsState = { ...toolSettingsState, isLoading: false, settings: {...} }` advances state, also bump `dataUpdatedAt` (e.g., → `1`).
3. **Do not** weaken `hasPersistedData = dbSettings !== undefined` in production code — the M7B contract is strict `!== undefined` per `useToolSettings`'s `T | undefined` return type. Fix the mock, not the contract.

## Phase 3: Fix overlay regressions (covers failures in files 5–6)

### Step 6: Tighten `getTopModalOverlay` to filter stale/closed modals using the repo's shared open-state rule (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** an inline helper `isPopupElementOpen` at the top of `overlayStack.ts` that mirrors `readOpenState` in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/useOpenFromDataState.ts:3-6` — returns `true` for `data-state === 'open'` OR `data-open === ''` OR `data-open === 'true'`. Add a one-line comment: `// Mirrors readOpenState in useOpenFromDataState.ts — keep in sync when adding new Base UI attribute variants.`
   ```ts
   function isPopupElementOpen(el: HTMLElement): boolean {
     // Mirrors readOpenState in useOpenFromDataState.ts — keep in sync.
     const dataState = el.getAttribute('data-state');
     const dataOpen = el.getAttribute('data-open');
     return dataState === 'open' || dataOpen === '' || dataOpen === 'true';
   }
   ```
2. **Update** `getTopModalOverlay` at `overlayStack.ts:88-97` to reverse-iterate and return the last overlay where `overlay.modal === true` **and** `overlay.elements.length > 0` **and** `overlay.elements.some(isPopupElementOpen)`. Stale modals (empty elements) and closed modals (no open attribute) are skipped.
3. **Consequence for other callers** (verified in this step): `getTopmostKnownModalOverlayType()` at `overlayDom.ts:87-88` and `hasKnownModalOverlay()` at `overlayDom.ts:92-93` both inherit the tightened semantics automatically (they proxy `getTopModalOverlay`). `syncBodyPointerEvents` at `overlayStack.ts:121-147` also inherits — which is the correct intent: stale modals should NOT strand body pointer-events. Verify `overlaySequences.test.tsx` still passes: those tests mount real Base UI popups whose attributes include `data-open=""`, so `isPopupElementOpen` returns true and body-pointer-events gating stays active.

### Step 7: Insert `pushOverlay` registrations at their DOM-order position (`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Root cause (narrow):** nested overlays (a menu containing a submenu) register via React effects in child-first order. Sibling overlays (dialog+popover, menu+dialog) register in the order the user opens them, which naturally matches DOM-mount order. The fix must preserve push-order-top for siblings but reorder only the nested case.
2. **Update** `pushOverlay` at `overlayStack.ts:173-183`: after filtering out any prior entry with the same id, find the **first existing entry whose popup element DOM-follows the new entry's popup element** (using `compareDocumentPosition`). If such an entry exists, insert the new entry **before** it. Otherwise append at the top (current behavior). Use the new entry's first popup-kind element; fall back to the first element if no popup-kind element is present; if the new entry has no elements yet, append.
   ```ts
   const newPopup = findPopupElement(nextEntry);
   const insertIndex = newPopup
     ? remainingOverlays.findIndex((o) => domFollows(findPopupElement(o), newPopup))
     : -1;
   const nextOverlays =
     insertIndex === -1
       ? [...remainingOverlays, nextEntry]
       : [...remainingOverlays.slice(0, insertIndex), nextEntry, ...remainingOverlays.slice(insertIndex)];
   ```
   where `domFollows(a, b)` returns `!!(a && b && a.compareDocumentPosition(b) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_PRECEDING)` — i.e., "b precedes a" means "a follows b in DOM".
3. **Do not change `updateOverlay`** — its documented contract is "preserves ordering position", and the bug is exclusively at initial push time. Element patches arrive after the initial registration and should not reorder the stack.
4. **Why this preserves sibling behavior:** when a second sibling overlay opens (e.g., popover after dialog), its popup mounts at a DOM position **after** the dialog's popup. `domFollows(dialogPopup, popoverPopup)` is false (popoverPopup doesn't precede dialogPopup), so no existing entry DOM-follows the new popover → `insertIndex === -1` → append at the top. Same as today. The only case where the new rule deviates is when the new push's popup is DOM-earlier than an already-registered popup — i.e., the nested parent-pushes-after-child case.
5. **Verify** via the targeted overlay tests: `lightbox.test.tsx`, `hover-card.test.tsx`, `tooltip.test.tsx`, `overlayBridge.test.tsx`, `overlaySequences.test.tsx` — all must stay green. The dropdown submenu case becomes green because when `DropdownMenuContent` (parent) pushes after `DropdownMenuSubContent` (child), the parent's popup DOM-precedes the child's → parent inserts at index 0 (before submenu) → stack = `[menu, menu-submenu]`.

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the proposed shared interaction-state location against the actual hook graph in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:194`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts:172-204`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.contexts.ts:72-166`. rev 3 says the ref should live in `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts`, but that file only memoizes context values while `useclipdrag`, `timelinecanvas`, and `usepinnedgroupsync` are all coordinated from `timelineeditor`, so the stated location and rationale are still technically off.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "refactor-pinned-shot-groups-20260408-0508"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-131",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-132",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 11.2 specifies the reverse switch is 'single commit, no `start`/`children`/`offset` reads anywhere' but doesn't state the mutation type explicitly. since the step describes metadeletes + cliporderoverride + pinnedshotgroupsoverride in a single commit, the mutation must be `type: 'rows'` (the only variant carrying all three). self-consistent with step 11.1's restriction on `type: 'pinnedshotgroups'`, but making the mutation type explicit would reduce executor ambiguity.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-135",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 3 against the actual `useselectedmediaclips()` inputs in [useselectedmediaclips.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). that hook currently reads only `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig` from editor context, so the plan's literal instruction to join `resolvedconfig.pinnedshotgroups` with `useshots()` data will not compile unless it first switches to `data?.config.pinnedshotgroups` or another real source of pinned groups.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-149",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual timeline selection ownership across `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelineselection.ts`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usemultiselect.ts`. `usetimelinecommit` owns only `selectedclipid` and `selectedtrackid`, while `usemultiselect` still owns `selectedclipids`, `primaryclipid`, and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan's step 3 is technically incomplete: following it literally would leave timeline selection split between the new store and old hook-local state instead of collapsing it into one authoritative store.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-150",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the current imperative selection adapters in `useclipdrag.ts`, `usetimelinepersistence.ts`, `usepollsync.ts`, and `videoeditorprovider.tsx`. the mutable refs (`selectedclipidsref`, `additiveselectionref`, `selectedclipidref`, `selectedtrackidref`) are runtime adapters for drag/save/poll callers rather than durable domain state, so the plan's wording about putting 'refs/helpers' into the `timeline` slice is a design error unless it explicitly keeps store state separate from ref adapters rebuilt around that state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-166",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` and `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/tracklistrenderer.tsx`. `timelinecanvas` still computes shared `rowresizepreview`, `selectedtrackid`, and other drag-sensitive props centrally and passes them through every `sortablerow`, so moving context reads to narrow zustand selectors alone will not meet the gate that non-dragged rows stay at 0 rerenders during drag unless the plan also changes this row-prop fan-out.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-167",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the staged-vs-mounted add flow in `src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useaddtovideoeditor.ts`. that hook decides between immediate in-process drop and staged url/localstorage fallback by testing whether the safe timeline hooks return `null`, so a global `timelinestore.ts` with default state would change runtime behavior unless the migration preserves an explicit 'editor mounted' availability boundary.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-168",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts` and `src/tools/video-editor/contexts/videoeditorprovider.tsx` together. the current composition is synchronous and consumers like the pending-add drain and agentchat bridge read fresh `editor` values in the same render pass, so the plan's 'build values and write them into the zustand store' step is under-specified about whether hydration happens during render or in an effect, and either choice has real correctness risk if not designed carefully.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.
